option.q;\n    const double T = option.t;\n    const double sigma = option.vol;\n\n    if (T <= 0.0 || sigma <= 0.0) {\n        return 0.0;\n    }\n\n    const double d1 = (log(S / K) + (r - q + 0.5 * __device__ double blackScholes(const OptionInput& option) noexcept {\n    const double S = option.spot;\n    const double K = option.strike;\n    const double r = option.r;\n    const double q = option.q;\n    const double T = option.t;\n    const double sigma = option.vol;\n\n    if (T <= 0.0 || sigma <= 0.0) {\n        return 0.0;\n    }\n\n    const double d1 = (log(S / K) + (r - q + 0.5 * generateOptions(std::vector<OptionInput>& options, const size_t numOptions) {\n    constexpr auto testOptions = getTestOptions();\n    options.resize(numOptions);\n\n    for (size_t i = 0; i <
